## Task 3.3 — RAM anchor/mapping Technical Implementation Contract amendment

Design RAM1–RAM8: AWAITING_GATE_2B_REVIEW.
SPECS_CHANGE_REQUIRED: NO. Current TIC: AWAITING_CONTROL_TOWER_REVIEW.
This adds acceptance obligations to existing Task 3.3 only; no new checkbox,
completed task, implementation authorization or actual authority is created.

1. Implement separate host observation, independently assigned logical repository
   anchor and approved host-to-/input projection mapping under RAM2/RAM3.
2. Enforce all closed schema fields/versions and noncircular external approval;
   reject missing/unknown fields, wrong scope, stale policy and self-binding.
3. Verify standalone physical root/Git/common/index relations with the existing
   pinned producer; linked/bare/alternate layouts explicitly fail closed.
4. Bind approved tracked/source/HEAD/index state to that same anchor; path/remote
   URL/repositoryKey matching alone never provides repository authority.
5. Construct secret-free exact authoritative projection after LC/LCN validation;
   no original full-tree bind/copy, unknown omission or .git/store delivery.
6. Prove actual external read-only mount configuration, exact source/target,
   disjoint scratch, no second root/writable physical alias/network/socket/store.
   Consumer assertion-only evidence is insufficient.
7. Bind approved mapping via GTA2 graph scope and REAL DA1 scope; retain 18 DA1
   fields. Keep 26 DA4 fields; use RAM5 logical anchor/scope identity rules.
8. Validate PRE/POST host binding, original ownership, source and delivered
   inventory independently, then compare stable projection. Validate dynamic
   observations separately against the same policy; no future evidence hash.
9. Prove all RAM7 failures plus valid standalone, wrong gitDir/commonDir/index,
   foreign repository, mount substitution, writable/alternate mapping, linked
   denial, synthetic anchor, wrong mapping scope, self-approved mapping,
   consumer symlink/reparse escape, secret delivery and unapproved relocation.
10. Prove approved host relocation can preserve logical anchor/graph identity
    while host-binding/fixed-state approvals are separately renewed. Do not
    equate two different physical roots within one invocation or silently move it.
11. Preserve one-per-target completeness and graph-wide input checks; controlled
    REAL fixture authorities never authorize YUTA. Actual smoke remains deferred
    until RAM6 dependency order and separate execution approval are satisfied.
12. Record exact commands, correct/incorrect/skipped counts, missing suites,
    source/schema/policy hashes and protected drift. Design PASS is not test PASS.

### Future technical implementation contract

1. Preserve the artifact lane, strict generic result schema and per-path
   classification. Artifact result sha256 remains the observed repository raw
   hash. Retain existing artifact extra-result behavior and Phase 1/2/3.1 checks.
2. Implement a separate source-owned generation registry and required-obligation
   derivation. The exact applicable V-FIX historical admission requires its
   separately approved generation obligation; omitted input must not erase the
   obligation. No optional caller policy, bypass flag or automatic empty set.

   version, owner/domain, approved generation and output identities and
   COLD_AND_REPEATED mode. Resolve the exact approval separately, outside the
   canonical identity body. No historical/current approval inheritance.
4. Use the separate GS4 generation result schema and evidence validation.
   Require fresh source/input/runtime/font/supply binding and complete cold plus
   repeated execution/output evidence. Digests and COMPLETE labels alone do not
   prove reproduction. Do not fabricate fields absent from renderer output.
5. Aggregate all required artifact obligations AND all required generation
   obligations. Preserve distinct observed universes even for equal logical
   paths. Never synthesize a repository artifact row for generation evidence.
6. Implement all GS5 failure codes and deterministic reporting. Missing,
   duplicate, extra/unknown, stale, malformed, partial, timed-out or skipped
   generation evidence fails closed. Preserve MB5 detailed failure causes.
7. Carry forward GS6 exact exit-1 classification. Test every premise negatively,
   including wrong mismatch membership despite correct counts. Exit 1 is never
   generic success; the historical comparison remains FAIL. Independent current
   reproduction may proceed only after the complete known signature is proven.
8. Preserve admission references and historical evidence. Before execution,

   Design/review bytes. Do not auto-rebaseline or rewrite prior approval units.

### Protected State and Evidence Protocol

Planning HEAD: `415990386327aaccab3c32b1fef0569a0fde7f3a`. Dirty checkout is not a clean baseline; preserve all unrelated tracked/untracked work. Capture fresh full path/raw-hash/status inventory before each phase and compare after it, not only `git diff` (untracked files must be included). Exact approved phase allowlist is the only permitted delta; all other files remain byte-identical. Appendix A/B original hashes never silently replaced by phase postimages; separately approved stage revisions retain both identities.

Parent tasks raw SHA-256: `36e66b08a712d99a12f3f3243a803bced9bf707a7bb6ea9b47b83ec7e896b504`.
Installed `.agents/skills/ui-ux-pro-max`: 70 files; receipt `installation.json` raw SHA-256 `1ff4b04f62e8c76ad6242ca0640f564618e4f3c89d18f767f6bf9cc6193ba1c7`; state `verified`; all 69 receipt-listed members match. Fresh planning 70-file snapshot SHA-256: `f834524b2631e4a670244dd66240046fcad22398d921ce28707d519953b8f490`. This observation does not replace any parent-approved digest convention. Snapshot algorithm: slash-relative paths sorted by JavaScript code-unit order; compact `JSON.stringify` of insertion-ordered path-to-raw-SHA object, UTF-8, no trailing newline. Protect all 70 files and receipt; never invoke install/resume/verify APIs or mutate parent evidence in this change.

Each task's evidence **E0**: task ID, exact approved scope, PRE/POST path/hash/status, command/cwd/tool identity, start/end, actual exit, raw output identity and observed assertions; distinguish PASS/FAIL/SKIPPED/NOT_RUN. Put task evidence here; final compliance/TECHNICAL VERIFY/QA in existing 03 final review. No new standalone evidence artifact without current workflow need and explicit review. Never rewrite historical failed commands. A test fixture approval is not real owner authorization.

### Recovery Contract

**R0 (every task):** On protected drift, missing authority, unexpected output/write or incomplete check, STOP with expected/current identity and keep task unchecked. No auto-rebaseline, blanket reset, rollback of unrelated work, or retry that broadens authority. In-memory/scratch proposals are disposable only after resolving absolute owned paths and verifying bounds; retain failure evidence first. Restoring an applied patch requires exact current postimage, exact reverse patch, owner authorization and preserved concurrent changes; otherwise STOP for recovery review.

**R1 (phases 1–3):** No operational route switch; failed candidate code/tests remain attributed, do not claim baseline remediation. Isolate native commands before execution; if source-write prevention cannot be proven on local/CI environment, STOP, not best-effort native execution.

**R2 (phase 4):** Candidate metadata/diffs remain unapproved until exact review. A stale candidate is rejected, never regenerated and accepted automatically. Keep H/A originals and original B ledger unchanged.

**R3 (phase 5):** Atomicity here is coherent reviewed file-set acceptance, not database work. Failure after an authorized subset never marks all migration complete; preserve original and approved postimage ledger separately. No manual repair of generated/live files; return to their owner/tooling review.

**R4 (phase 6):** Partial cutover must FAIL. Do not fallback to old OR new successful checks. Request exact rollback of package/ignore/policy/doc bundle against verified postimages; historical command failures stay failures. Parent remains held even if rollback succeeds.

### Technical Implementation Contract — Global

Each clause requires explicit PASS evidence before phase acceptance; UNKNOWN/NOT_RUN is not PASS.

| ID    | Binding contract                                                                          | Verification / evidence                                    |
| ----- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------- |
| TC-G1 | Exact approved seven artifacts, parent, installed70, unrelated sources protected          | Fresh raw PRE/POST map; no unauthorized delta              |
| TC-G2 | Only phase-authorized allowlist; no source/UI/runtime/auth/DB/product change              | Exact changed-path comparison, scoped diff, owner approval |
| TC-G3 | Six classes; R4 reproducible distinct from R4L live; every obligation routed              | N01–N05, N11, N17, N26, N33                                |
| TC-G4 | Exact67/45 admission; no permanent count cap, no automatic rebaseline                     | Appendices, full parser universe, N07–N09, N29             |
| TC-G5 | Generated raw outputs unchanged; no install/update/generate/migrate merely for validation | Read-only sandbox evidence, N13–N23, N34–N36               |

### Canonical Identity Corrective Evidence

Mode PHASE_1_CORRECTIVE_REMEDIATION_ONLY; Control Tower confirmed CANONICAL_IDENTITY_INFORMATION_LOSS. Phase1 historically passed50 then-current tests; Phase2 precheck discovered lost data before hashing, not a SHA-256 collision. Historical PASS/source hashes above are unchanged records. This corrective implementation replaces current foundation; tasks stay4/29, Phase2 BLOCKED pending review.

Impact inventory: canonicalJson → unitSha256; verifyUnit hashes parsed observed unit; admitEntry binds policy/entry/lifecycle bodies. parseJson performs duplicate-key-aware lexical precheck then JSON.parse; parsePolicy/policySchema and Zod reference/approval schemas validate semantic input. rawSha256 separately hashes exact byte buffers. There is no production identity writer: canonical hashes are synthetic in-memory test fixture refs; tests' scratch snapshots use rawSha256. No policy.json/preservation.json/migration-67.json exists, no real classification registration or external call site established by this change. Source/test and migration evidence are independently computed raw file hashes; installed70 snapshot uses its separately documented algorithm.

BUGGY_CANONICAL_HASHES_PERSISTED: NO (no persisted reviewed policy/classification identity or externally referenced identity produced by this module). No rebind/migration required. Existing synthetic ordinary fixture hashes remain compatible; prototype-like-key fixtures now retain all represented members.

Root cause: recursive z.record parsing produced a transformed object that dropped own **proto** before encoding. Correction: canonicalJson traverses original own enumerable JSON data descriptors directly, encodes keys without object merging/assignment, ignores inherited properties, rejects accessors/cycles/excess depth, and retains approved safe-integer scalar constraints. Zod validates scalar JSON values and policy semantics; its transformed object is never used as representation identity. admitEntry separately retains original parsed policy/entry/lifecycle bodies for hashing; verifyUnit already receives parsed representation. Duplicate JSON key rejection is explicitly required by Design D1 and remains unchanged. No new parser or broader equivalence was introduced.

### Real Command and Tooling QA Plan

- Proposed new test command after source authorization: `node --test scripts/format-policy/check.test.mjs` (not an existing passing command). Tests bind closed adapters and exercise N01–N37. Real reproduction/native runs must also PASS; adapter stubs alone never close 3.x.
- Current repository checks after implementation: `pnpm docs:check`, `pnpm architecture:check`, `pnpm typegen:next` then `pnpm -r --if-present typecheck`; strict `openspec validate repository-format-policy-and-baseline-remediation --strict --no-interactive`; scoped Prettier and diff checks on actual allowed mutable delivery paths. Do not format generated/H/A/live targets.
- Before activation the current global `pnpm format:check` remains the existing command and inherited failures remain attributed. Candidate global contract is exercised in faithful isolated scratch with reviewed package/ignore/policy bytes. Do not relabel scratch result as real repository PASS.
- After separately authorized activation, require actual repository `pnpm format:check` exit 0 with current full classification, mutable and every required alternate PASS. Test `pnpm format` as read-only under the approved Design; compare PRE/POST to prove no source writes. No repository-wide formatter write.
- Reproduction supply: approved OpenSpec 1.11.0 source/transformer closure, exact expected nine outputs; V-FIX pinned renderer/font/Python closure plus v1/v2 corpus; V-LOCK pnpm 11.8.0 native parser/writer raw roundtrip plus isolated frozen graph consistency. No latest/download/install/scripts/auto-update inferred by this plan; unavailable supply blocks relevant task.
- Live metadata: current Design F3 Drizzle Kit 0.31.10 native PostgreSQL check, safe existing-metadata mirror and reader, no repository config loading/DB credentials/DB connection. Require terminal completion + full raw outputs and no disallowed diagnostics, not just exit0. Native unsupported-version exit0 and missing-meta auto-create paths must be rejected. Prove source-write isolation for local and CI; if unavailable STOP. Bounded G1–G6 cover only accepted inventory/structure/order/reference/completion/reviewed-identity gaps, no SQL semantic engine or UUID/timestamp reproduction.
- Existing repository/native owner checks must run where applicable; no fabricated build/lint command. Tests/builds for sw1 are selected from the current POS manifest at its review, with exact commands recorded before mutation approval. General cloud/local builds and database suites are not substitutes for tooling QA; no database or production operation is authorized. If extra setup/scope is required, stop for approval.
- Command QA must execute actual fail-closed entry against isolated invalid inventories, broad ignores, stale approval, missing validator, timeouts, partial native output, interrupted migration and rollback candidates. Negative command must fail for the expected reason with a real nonzero exit, not merely compare a mock result object.
- Native reviewed-live PASS must include each of cloud/POS/Display scope, linkage and SQL presence under its accepted contract. Keep R4G output and R4L live result separate in aggregate audit.
- Run logical-determinism comparison twice with same state; safe audit metadata differs only in permitted incidental fields. Inspect output for secret/private contents without printing secrets.
- TECHNICAL VERIFY: 26 TIC clauses (8 global + 18 phase), 13 requirements / 77 scenarios and all29 task outputs. QA: real tooling command, bypass, migration-state and recovery evidence. Both required; QA NOT_APPLICABLE is forbidden. UI/browser testing is not required absent an approved scope change.

### Representation and authority

Historical strict legacyPolicySchema retains (1,1), rejecting V2-only fields.
Current parsePolicy recognizes valid legacy shape then fails
LEGACY_POLICY_VERSION_REQUIRES_MIGRATION. Mixed pairs fail
POLICY_VERSION_PAIR_MISMATCH; missing/malformed/future values fail
POLICY_VERSION_MISMATCH. V2 requires (2,2), domainAdmissions and ignoreMembership,
with strict unknown-key validation and unchanged six classes. No default/upgrade.

Domain admissions bind exact path, owner authority, type/reason evidence,
expected raw bytes, independent type/approval references and policy revision.
Type units bind V2 and formatter/config identities. Policy approval hashes the
original complete body, domain approval hashes the original admission excluding
only its reference. No real admission records were created. Parser-null remains
UNRESOLVED without valid independent evidence; supported/expected in-domain
members cannot be admitted out through the domain resolver.

Ignore membership binds source path/hash, Prettier algorithm/version, tool/config
identities, exact unique case-safe tracked paths, set digest and approval.
Actual membership comes from bound Prettier file-info semantics over independent
tracked inventory, excluding only valid domain-admitted OUT members. Unresolved
members stay accounted. Duplicate/extra/missing paths fail; same source with new
members is IGNORE_SCOPE_DRIFT; source/tool/config staleness is IGNORE_BINDING_STALE.
Sorted membership set identity is explicit; whole-policy raw representation
identity preserves original array order. Neither ignore nor parser-null supplies
authority. Ignored mutable files still execute direct Prettier; routing mismatch
remains IGNORE_POLICY_MISMATCH, never silent exclusion or grandfathering.

Original parsed JSON identity remains separate from Zod models. V1/V2 identity,
prototype-like keys, own-property traversal and duplicate-key regressions pass.

### SOURCE_CLOSURE_COMPOSITE_ALGORITHM

1. Hash exact raw bytes of each member with SHA-256, lowercase hexadecimal.
2. Use normalized package-relative paths as unique object keys and their raw
   hashes as string values.
3. Sort paths ascending using JavaScript default lexical code-unit ordering.
4. Serialize the sorted object with JSON.stringify, no indentation.
5. Encode as UTF-8, without BOM or trailing newline, and SHA-256 those bytes.

JSON key/value quoting and delimiters provide unambiguous path/content framing.
No content normalization occurs. Independent verification manually framed the
same sorted JSON key/value pairs and used WebCrypto SHA-256 instead of the
primary createHash calculation; both matched.

RECOMPUTED_SOURCE_CLOSURE_SHA256:
`945dadf7ff9c1746c2dacd0e47e94b11c044f931704a9ffeb913d9fc57f42e19`.

target paths and reproduced raw-byte hashes. Transformer subclosure uses the
same algorithm over exactly `dist/utils/command-references.js` and its imported
`dist/core/command-generation/invocation.js`; their individual hashes are above.

Actual generator entrypoints are getSkillTemplates and generateSkillContent in
`dist/core/shared/skill-generation.js`. Actual template source paths are the
template facade and workflow members in the exact closure table.

The source transformer recognizes `/opsx:([a-z-]+)` command references, maps
known workflow identifiers to skill names, and emits Codex/other-agent forms.
For example, `/opsx:apply` becomes
`$openspec-apply-change (Codex) or /openspec-apply-change (other agents)`.
Unknown references remain unchanged. This behavior was inspected in the exact
hashed transformer source, not inferred from output alone. The command-surface
resolver/update integration was inspected as context but is not invoked by this
explicit-input reproduction or included silently in the candidate closure.

### Execution, audit and isolation

Actual stages: select nine templates; select Codex skills/skills-invocable
transformer; transform template instructions inside generateSkillContent
BEFORE frontmatter assembly; assemble with exact 1.11.0 version; compare UTF-8
raw bytes. No newline/BOM/Unicode/whitespace normalization or formatter pass.

The adapter reads exact sources, copies only verified members to a unique
external temporary directory, verifies that snapshot, and executes the pinned
pure pipeline in a bounded Node child process. It does not invoke update/init.
Worker output is memory only; 30-second maximum timeout, 2 MiB output limit,
no inherited NODE_OPTIONS or private environment content. Cleanup resolves and
checks only the exact owned scratch directory. Source/target raw identities are
checked again after execution; no persistent PASS cache.

Adapter-specific structured evidence is returned separately as evidence from
validateGeneratedArtifacts: closure, tool version, transformer, cold scope,
per-target expected/observed/reproduced identities and result. Its six-field
results alone enter generic aggregation. This Tasks table retains diagnostics
under D9's owner-controlled review evidence; no generic audit/schema widening
or repository automatic report writer.

Warm: PASS (second generation in that same child).

### Environmental inputs and pipeline

| Input                          | Current/source value                                                    | Output relevance / MUST_BIND                                                               |
| ------------------------------ | ----------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| Locale                         | fr_FR / cp1252                                                          | No explicit locale formatting; transitive library relevance unresolved / UNCERTAIN         |
| Timezone                       | Romance Standard Time / Romance Daylight Time                           | invariant=1 requests stable PDF metadata; installed implementation unavailable / UNCERTAIN |
| Encoding                       | Python and filesystem UTF-8; explicit manifest UTF-8                    | Serialization / YES                                                                        |
| OS/newlines                    | Windows; Path.write_text has no newline override                        | Manifest LF text may be written as CRLF / YES                                              |
| Font discovery                 | First existing literal path above                                       | Scan glyphs/raster output / YES                                                            |
| Python/library/native versions | Candidate Python, missing Pillow/ReportLab                              | Rasterization, JPEG, compression, PDF serialization / YES                                  |

Actual pipeline:

1. Parse corpus version (default v2), build source-defined fixture/answer list.
2. For v2, read v1 manifest and resolve frozen PDF paths by fixture ID.
3. Existing main clears TEMP and existing output PDFs: do not invoke on actual tree.

   transforms; save JPEG with explicit options, then drawImage into PDF.
7. ReportLab saves A4 compressed invariant PDF with fixed title/author.
8. Hash resulting PDF bytes; assemble manifest fields in insertion/list order.
9. json.dumps(indent=2, ensure_ascii=True) + newline, write_text UTF-8; cleanup TEMP.

### Derivation scope and method

Exact source: apps/backoffice/scripts/generate-personnel-contract-evaluation-corpus.py,
SHA-256 6ddbd29046cbff645bd546e472f04cac010d7e6d53c84ed9a67f0ca04201bff2.
The existing 59-input closure remains
35e3ff1fd1cef6d6f336b155d5b6bec7bee679ea6e12666e838f6cc78c46b9d5.
The 122-output closure remains
a075ef0e47cfa288a93316c99d28bb1b74bab6362e081ded4b18e17a62f40560.

Analysis runs Python -B via stdin using the discovery runtime only, not canonical
authority. Parse source AST; select only literal assignments WATERMARK,
V2_CHANGED_FIXTURE_IDS, STARTER_FIXTURES, POSITIONS, HOURS and the reviewed pure
functions term_text, resolved_fixture, degraded_fixture, adversarial_fixture,
build_fixtures. Evaluate those data builders in memory. Do NOT import the
generator module, call main, find_font, scan_image, write_fixture, Image.new,
canvas.Canvas or any write/render function. No image or PDF is created.

Evaluate build_fixtures in order v1 then v2; each produces 60 fixture records.
For v1, derive draw requests for every fixture/page. For v2, derive only
wg2-adversarial-05 and wg2-adversarial-09. The other 58 PDFs are byte-copy inputs,
not additional draw requests. Their manifest values control copy/hash matching,
not rendered text. Existing images are never OCR'd or counted as new text.
Thus 62 PDF-generation branches, 58 copy branches and two JSON manifests account
for 122 outputs; 122 outputs does not mean 122 newly rendered PDFs.

For each page retain source fixture order and page order. For scan pages append
watermark, page caption, each textwrap.wrap(line,width=58) result in line order,
and scan footer. The regular face is used at 20/28/34 points, without synthetic
bold. For digital pages append watermark, fixture/page header, each
textwrap.wrap(line,width=82) result with first line bold and others Roman, then
oblique footer. Use standard textwrap defaults from the discovery Python 3.12.14.
No style or string substitutions are invented. Conditional data-builder branches
(number modulo, term, positions/hours and v1/v2 wording) are evaluated exactly.
Future runtime/source changes must revalidate this derivation.

categories: wrapped fixture content CORPUS, formatted headers/captions
GENERATED_FORMATTING, watermark/footer STATIC_LITERAL. CORPUS includes the
pure data builders' formatting, not only literal source text.
Hash serialization: Python json.dumps(records, ensure_ascii=True,
sort_keys=True, separators=(',', ':')), UTF-8 without BOM/trailing newline.
No private data or full synthetic text dump is needed.

The following object includes all 8 extension modules, 18 bundled native
libraries, their exact sizes/SHA/ELF type/SONAME/DT_NEEDED, and base library
identities. For every consuming module/library, resolve each DT_NEEDED name
first to the exact pillowNative SONAME (BUNDLED_WHEEL), otherwise to
baseLibraries SONAME (BASE_IMAGE). This deterministic join resolved all 90
Pillow DT_NEEDED edges, with zero unresolved names and no OTHER source.
Transitive base library DT_NEEDED entries are also resolved in this inventory.
FreeType, JPEG, PNG, TIFF, LCMS, WebP, HarfBuzz, Brotli, AVIF, OpenJPEG, XCB,
Xau, LZMA and Zstd are actually wheel-bundled; zlib and system runtime libraries
resolve to the base. This is static ELF/SONAME closure, not execution of the
uninstalled wheels or proof of all dynamic-loading/symbol behavior.

scan raster/encoding pipeline, not an exact historical runtime identification.

- Different ReportLab/PDF serialization settings: LOW. For: resource names and

- Different generator content/code: LOW. For: historical exact execution source

### Candidate stacks and ranked recovery

Historical Python/Pillow/ReportLab/FreeType/JPEG exact versions UNKNOWN.
Version ranges from requirements are constraints only, not installed evidence.

1. R2 historical font reconstruction: highest relative priority, MEDIUM
   confidence. Arial on Windows or DejaVu on Linux are source-supported
   candidates; current Vera is explicitly injected. Text-correlated differences
   support font/raster divergence. Neither candidate's exact bytes are proven.
   Need historical OS/execution record or approved candidate font identities.
   Replacing canonical Vera requires Design review; diagnostic-only trial
   requires separate authorization, not a current runtime change.
2. R3 native raster/JPEG reconstruction: MEDIUM plausibility. FreeType/hinting,
   antialiasing, Pillow resampling and JPEG DCT rounding could contribute.
   Need exact historical wheel/build/native metadata. JPEG headers provide no
   version discriminator. Canonical native change requires Design/supply review.
3. R1 historical Pillow reconstruction: LOW-to-MEDIUM. Requirements permit
   multiple versions but identify none; no version-specific behavior found.
   Need lock/RECORD/log evidence first. Canonical version change requires review.
4. R4 full historical runtime: plausible fallback, costly and broad. Need
   archived environment/container and font provenance; current evidence cannot
   identify a complete stack. Canonical replacement requires Design review.
5. R5 insufficient evidence: remains possible for exact-byte recovery if
   historical artifacts are unavailable. No impossibility conclusion.
